                                                              Chairman’s Statement

2022 年 世 界 和 中 國 都 面 臨 艱 難 的 試 煉，疫 情       The world and China faced a difficult trial in 2022 given the
帶來前所未見的考驗，統一企業中國控股有限                       unprecedented challenges brought by the pandemic. Amidst such
公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」                    uncertainties, Uni-President China Holdings Ltd. (the “Company”)
或「我們」）持續專注在不確定的環境中，投資                      and its subsidiaries (together as the “Group”, “our Group”, “we”, “our”
未來、創造價值、管理風險和提高彈性，進入                       or “us”) continued to focus on investing in future, creating value,
增 長 的 新 階 段；本 集 團 自 2019 年 以 來，收 益         managing risk and increasing resilience, through which we have
年複合增長率達 8.7%，其中飲品和食品的年                     entered a new phase of growth. Since 2019, the Group’s revenue
收益複合增長率，分別為 8.8% 和 9.0%。中國                 has been growing at a compound annual growth rate of 8.7%, in
14 億 人 口 對 於 安 全、美 味、健 康 的 優 質 食 品         which the compound annual growth rate of the beverages and food
及飲料的需求，發展空間仍巨大，除了中產階                       reached 8.8% and 9.0% respectively. The demand for safe, tasty,
級持續壯大，帶動對於更優質、更健康的產品                       healthy and quality food and beverages from China’s 1.4 billion
的追尋，大眾對於個性化、便利性和價值的消                       population remains tremendous with plenty of room for development.
費需求，更能帶動集團收益增長的可持續性。                       In addition to the growing middle class, which is driving the demand
                                           for better quality and healthier products, the public’s pursuit for
                                           personalization, convenience and value in consumption is also
                                           conducive to the sustainable growth of the Group’s revenue.

2022 年也是本集團進入中國大陸市場的三十                     The year of 2022 also marked the 30th anniversary of the Group’s
周年，收益再創新高，達人民幣 28,257.4 百萬                 entry into the Mainland China market, with revenue reaching a new
元，較去年增長 12.0%。本集團全體三萬多名                    record of RMB28,257.4 million, representing an increase of 12.0%
員工和衷共濟，創建新方式來吸引新客戶的創                       over last year. The Group’s 30,000+ employees have exhibited their
業 心 態 強，於 挑 戰 之 中 保 持 穩 健 經 營 並 尋 求        strong entrepreneurship to work together to create new ways of
發展機遇；並對於新市場謹慎測試並管理風險，                      attracting customers, and manage to maintain a stable operation
創新是本集團持續推動增長的必經之路。                         and seek growth opportunities in the midst of challenges. In addition,
                                           the Group has been careful in testing and managing risks in new
                                           markets. Innovation remains an imperative driver of the Group’s
                                           continuous growth.

本集團持續聚焦經營，構建彈性供應鏈，聚焦                       The Group continued to focus on its operation and formulate core
品牌力建設，鮮度管理為經營的基本政策，並                       operation policies to build a flexible supply chain, concentrate on
拓展線上、線下渠道，貼近理解消費者，建立                       brand building and freshness control. We also expanded our online
有黏性的生態系統，進一步提升滲透率。本集                       and offline channels to get a deeper understanding of consumers
團 將 繼 續 以 滿 足 消 費 者 需 求 為 宗 旨，力 求 透        and build an adhesive ecosystem to further enhance penetration.
過品牌力的建設來構築品牌長期的競爭壁壘，                       With the aim to satisfy consumers’ needs, the Group will make
透 過 消 費 者 需 求 洞 察 驅 動 的 產 品 創 新，及 圍        continuous efforts to build a long-term competitive barrier of brands
繞消費者場景與觸點運營，秉持「誠實勤道、                       through the building of brand power, and to carry out product
創新求進」的經營理念，堅持「價值營銷」，聚                      innovation driven by consumer demand insight and centering
焦經營，致力打造品牌價值，穩健踏實走自己                       on consumer scenarios and contacts. The Group adheres to
的 路，以「健 康 操 作」循 序 漸 進 追 求 進 步，致            the corporate vision of “Honesty and Diligence; Innovation and
力成為一個具備「品格」、「品牌」與「品味」的                     Excellence ( 誠 實 勤 道、創 新 求 進 )” and upholds the principle of
三品企業。                                      “Value Marketing ( 價 值 營 銷 )”. With a focus on operation, we are
                                           dedicated to creating brand value, pursuing steady development and
                                           striving for progressive advancement by “Healthy Practice ( 健 康 操
                                           作 )”, with an aim to evolve into an enterprise boasting “Integrity ( 品

                                 Management Discussion & Analysis

經濟環境分析                                     ANALYSIS ON ECONOMIC ENVIRONMENT

中華人民共和國（「中國」）2022 年國內生產總                   In 2022, the gross domestic product (“GDP”) of the People’s
值 (GDP) 同比增長 3.0%，社會消費品零售總額                Republic of China (the “PRC” or “China”) recorded a year-on-year
同 比 下 降 0.2%。面 對 複 雜 嚴 峻 的 地 緣 政 治、        increase of 3.0% and total retail sales of consumer goods declined
經 濟 環 境 和 國 內 疫 情 反 復 多 變 等 多 重 考 驗，       by 0.2% year-on-year. In the face of multiple difficulties, including a
中 國 政 府 適 時 調 整 和 優 化 防 疫 政 策，減 少 對        complex and grim geopolitical and economic environment and the
生產、生活的影響，以促進市場回暖與保障民                       recurrent pandemic in China, the PRC government has adjusted
生 消 費，雖 然 整 體 消 費 市 場 在 疫 情 的 衝 擊 下        and optimized its pandemic prevention policies in a timely manner,
仍 顯 疲 弱，作 為 民 生 基 本 需 求 的 食 品 飲 料 行        reducing the impact on production and daily lives with a view to
業 保 持 恢 復 發 展 態 勢。中 國 提 出 雙 循 環 戰 略        promoting market recovery and protecting people’s livelihoods
部屬，建構國內大循環為主體，國內國際雙循                       and consumption. Although the overall consumer market remains
環 相 互 促 進 的 新 發 展 格 局，國 內 循 環 重 點 是        weak under the impact of the pandemic, the food and beverage
擴大內需，完善產業體系，形成以消費拉動為                       industry, as a basic necessity for people’s livelihoods, has
主 的 增 長 模 式，推 動 經 濟 實 現 再 平 衡。2023         maintained its recovery momentum. The PRC has proposed a dual
年 中 國 經 濟 增 長 預 期 穩 中 求 進，著 力 擴 大 國        circulation strategy to build a new development pattern in which
內需求，推動產業鏈、供應鏈循環暢通，居民                       the domestic circulation is the mainstay, and the domestic and
消費逐步回歸常態。                                  international circulation are mutually reinforcing, with the domestic
                                           circulation focusing on expanding domestic demand and improving
                                           the industrial system, so as to form a growth model driven by
                                           consumption and promote economic rebalancing. In 2023, China
                                           is expected to witness a stable economic growth, with focuses on
                                           expanding domestic demand, promoting a smooth industrial and
                                           supply chain cycle, and gradually returning consumer spending to
                                           normal level.

原物料採購方面，也面臨了多年來最艱難的處                       In terms of sourcing raw materials, we also faced the toughest
境，本集團運用在復雜、不確定和快速變化的                       situation in years. Thanks to the close cooperation of our teams, the
環境中，透過緊密結合的團隊，調整供應鏈，                       Group managed to adjust its supply chain in a complex, uncertain
就長遠來看，這種敏捷性需要擴展到研發、採                       and fast-changing environment. In the long run, such agility needs
購、規劃、製造和物流，更敏捷的市場需求感                       to be extended to R&D, sourcing, planning, manufacturing and
知 及 動 態 預 測，將 成 為 日 常 供 應 鏈 運 營 的 重        logistics. We need to be more sensitive to market demand and
要 組 成 部 分，並 在 更 深 層 次 上 了 解 大 宗 商 品        respond faster to dynamic forecast, which shall serve as a crucial
波動性及風險。                                    component of daily supply chain operations, as well as to have a
                                           deeper understanding of commodity volatility and risk.

    Management Discussion & Analysis

疫情封控期間，O2O、社區電商、社會團購等                         During the lockdown period, new consumption channels such as
新 型 消 費 渠 道 帶 來 新 的 行 銷 機 遇。複 雜 多 變           O2O, community e-commerce and community group purchase
的外部環境使得消費者在日常購買決策中開                           have brought new marketing opportunities. The complex and
始 審 視 自 己 的 真 實 需 求，渴 望 擁 有 更 健 康 的           changing external environment has led consumers to reflect on their
生活。隨著疫情防控政策優化，消費者的生活                          real needs and desire for a healthier life in their daily purchasing
習慣和消費價值觀也發生轉變，健康、營養、                          decisions. With the optimization of pandemic prevention and control
高 品 質 為 消 费 需 求 增 加，越 來 越 快 的 市 場 變           policies, consumers’ lifestyle and their views on consumption have
化 代 表 全 渠 道 的 重 要 性 日 益 增 加。本 集 團 秉           also changed, whereby demands for healthy, nutritious and quality
持價值營銷策略，積極順應消費趨勢的變化，                          products increased. As the market changes increasingly accelerate,
滿足不同場景下消費者的多元化需求。                             the use of omnichannel is more important than ever. The Group
                                              upholds a value-based marketing strategy and actively responds
                                              to changes in consumer trends to meet their diversified needs in
                                              different scenarios.


未來展望                                          PROSPECT

本集團始終著眼於長期目標做出決策和投資，                          The Group always makes decisions and investments in light of our
持續有效的激勵措施，在短、中、長期戰略之                          long-term goals, continuously implements effective incentives to
間取得平衡。本集團將持續聚焦經營，在「快、                         strike a balance among short-, medium- and long-term strategies.
穩、鮮」經營方針的指導下，又快又穩地實現                          The Group will continue to focus on its operation and, under the
經營目標，爭取更高的經營績效。                               guidance of our operating direction of being “quick, stable and fresh
                                              ( 快、穩、鮮 )”, we will achieve our business objectives swiftly and
                                              steadily, striving for better operating results.


永續發展                                          SUSTAINABLE DEVELOPMENT

本集團以人為本，追求可持續的增長，積極回                          The Group pursues sustainable growth with a people-oriented
應「推 動 綠 色 發 展，促 進 人 與 自 然 和 諧 共 生」            approach by actively responding to the national strategy of
的國家戰略，考量全球各項環境議題的最新發                          “promoting green development for the harmonious coexistence
展趨勢，承擔保護環境的社會責任；對於廢水、                         between humanity and nature”, taking into account the latest
能源、水、溫室氣體、有害及無害廢棄物，均                          development of various global environmental issues and undertaking

食品安全                                       FOOD SAFETY

本集團秉持「三好一公道」的理念，即品質好、                      We uphold the philosophy of “Three Goods and One Fairness ( 三
信 用 好、服 務 好、價 格 公 道，以「吃 得 安 心、             好 一 公 道 )”, i.e. Good Quality, Good Credit, Good Service and
吃得開心」為使命，向消費者提供安全、健康、                      Fair Price ( 品 質 好、信 用 好、服 務 好、價 格 公 道 ), and with the
美 味 的 食 品。本 集 團 成 立 以 總 經 理 為 主 任 的        mission of producing products that consumers will “be comfortable
食 安 品 質 委 員 會，作 為 食 品 安 全 和 品 質 系 統        and happy to eat ( 吃 得 安 心、吃 得 開 心 )”, we offer safe, healthy
設計、優化的最高權力組織，其委員全面覆蓋                       and delicious food to consumers. We have set up the Food Safety
商業研發所、採購資源管理室、生產管理群及                       and Quality Committee with our General Manager as its Director,
食 品 安 全 管 理 室 等 相 關 部 門，定 期 舉 行 會 議        as the organization of supreme power to design and optimise the
對食品安全管理事項進行研討。此外，本集團                       food safety and quality system. The members of the Food Safety
設立食品安全管理室為食品安全的管理部門，                       and Quality Committee cover all relevant departments including the
負 責 食 品 安 全 保 障 系 統 的 布 建、食 品 安 全 政        business research institute, procurement resources management
策的制定及落實，深化食品安全風險評估、預                       office, the production management group, food safety and
警及監控等。                                     management office, etc. The Committee holds meetings periodically
                                           to discuss food safety management issues. Besides, we have set

                                           safety risk assessment, provide warning and monitoring, etc.

本集團建立食品安全三道防線，即資質審查、                       We have established three lines of defense for food safety, namely,

商 的 食 品 安 全 進 行 管 理，有 效 控 制 供 應 商 帶        managing suppliers’ food safety from multiple perspectives, we have
入的食品安全風險，確保供應鏈食品安全。                        effectively controlled the food safety risk brought by suppliers and
                                           ensured food safety on the supply chain.

有效管理產品品質，預防品質異常的發生。本                       acceptance check, control of production process, and quality control
集團產品除嚴格遵守《中華人民共和國食品安                       of finished products. Our products strictly abide by relevant laws and

部 食 品 安 全 專 案 嚴 格 執 行 監 控，確 保 產 品 符        of China. Besides, the food safety solutions for all products are

 銷資產，以應對存在的風險變數，紮實前進。                        conditions, and making appropriate capital expenditures to optimise

庫務政策                                       Treasury Policy

本集團之庫務政策是不參與高風險之投資或                        It was the Group’s treasury management policy not to engage in
投 機 性 衍 生 工 具，亦 不 會 將 流 動 資 金 投 資 於        any highly risky investment or speculative derivative product and
具有重大相關槓桿效應或風險的金融產品上，                       not to invest the working capital in financial products with significant
包括對沖基金或類似的金融產品。本年度，本                       underlying leverage or risks, including hedge funds or similar
集團於財務風險管理方面繼續維持審慎態度。                       financial products. The Group continued to adopt a conservative
由 於 本 集 團 大 部 分 收 入 源 自 中 國 的 業 務，故        approach to financial risk management during the Year. Most of
大部分資產收款及付款貨幣均以人民幣計值。                       the Group’s receipts and payments were denominated in Renminbi
本集團若因以其他幣值資產或負債（如現金及                       since a majority of its revenue was derived from operations in the
現金等價物及借款）而承受外匯風險時，將適                       PRC. The Group may use foreign exchange forward contracts,
時運用外匯遠期合約進行風險的規避。                          when appropriate, for risk aversion when it is exposed to foreign
                                           exchange risk arising from assets or liabilities, such as cash and cash
                                           equivalents and borrowings, which may be denominated in other
                                           currencies.

 管治對於提高投資者對本公司的信心具有重                        that good corporate governance is crucial to enhance investors’
 要 意 義。本 公 司 董 事（「董 事」）會（「董 事 會」）           confidence in the Company. In the opinion of the board (the “Board”)
 認為，本公司於截至 2022 年 12 月 31 日止年度              of directors (the “Directors”) of the Company, the Company has

                                            Corporate Governance Report

董事之委任及重選                                   Appointment and Re-election of Directors

董事（包括獨立非執行董事）的任職期為三年。                      The term of office of the Directors (including independent non-
根據企業管治守則，獲委任以填補臨時空缺或                       executive Directors) is three years. In accordance with the Corporate
成 為 董 事 會 新 成 員 之 新 任 董 事，須 於 獲 委 任        Governance Code, any new Director appointed to fill a casual
後本公司首個股東大會上提呈本公司股東重選。                      vacancy or as an addition to the Board shall submit himself/herself
                                           for re-election by shareholders of the Company at the first general
                                           meeting of the Company after the appointment.

根 據 本 公 司 組 織 章 程 之 規 定，於 本 公 司 每 屆        In accordance with the articles of association of the Company, at
股 東 週 年 大 會（「股 東 週 年 大 會」）上，三 分 之          each annual general meeting of the Company (the “AGM”), one-
一在任董事（或董事並非三或三之倍數，則為                       third of the Directors for the time being or, if their number is not
最接近但不少於三分之一之人數）須輪值退任，                      three or a multiple of three, the number nearest to, but not less
而每位董事（包括有指定任期之董事）至少每                       than, one-third, shall retire from office by rotation provided that
三 年 輪 值 退 任 一 次。董 事 的 酬 金 是 根 據 董 事        every Director (including those appointed for a specific term) shall
提名政策（定義見下文）及董事會成員多元化                       be subject to retirement by rotation at least once every three years.
政策（定義見下文）、董事資格、職責、責任及                      The Directors’ remunerations are determined with reference to
經驗以及當時市況而定。                                the Director Nomination Policy (as defined below) and the Board
                                           Diversity Policy (as defined below), the Directors’ qualifications,
                                           duties, responsibilities and experiences, and the prevailing market
                                           conditions.

董事會的職位及責任                                  Roles and Responsibilities of the Board

董事會負責領導及掌控本公司，監督本集團業                       The Board is responsible for the leadership and control of the
務、投資及戰略決策、維持本集團有效的風險                       Company and oversees the Group’s businesses, investment and
管理及內部控制系統，監督本集團的表現。董                       strategic decisions, maintaining the effectiveness of the Group’s

已審閱本公司對企業管治守則是否遵例及有                          and (iv) developing, reviewing and monitoring the code of conduct
否於企業管治報告中披露。                                 and compliance manual applicable to employees of the Group and

持 最 高 水 平 之 企 業 管 治，以 及 認 同 並 確 信 董        and to maintain the highest standards of corporate governance and
事會成員多元化之裨益。                                recognises and embraces the benefits of having a diverse Board
                                           composition.

根 據 董 事 會 成 員 多 元 化 政 策，為 了 達 到 在 董        Under the Board Diversity Policy, in order to achieve a diversity of
事會內有多樣化的觀點、技能及經驗，在決定                       perspectives, skills and experience within the Board, a number of
任何人選加入董事會及繼續委任時將考慮多                        factors will be considered when deciding on appointments to the
項因素，包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化及教                       Board and the continuation of those appointments, including but
育背景、服務年期、技能、專業經驗、地區及                       not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational background,
行業經驗。在形成多樣化的觀點時，本公司亦                       length of service, skills, professional experience, regional and
將計及其本身的業務模式及不時的特定需要。                       industry experience. In forming its perspectives on Board diversity,
                                           the Company will also take into account its own business model and
                                           specific needs from time to time.

 風險管理及內部監控                                  RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL

 董事會整體負責維持本集團穩健而有效之風                        The Board has overall responsibilities for maintaining a sound and
 險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 系 統。該 系 統 包 含 界 定 且        effective risk management and internal control system of the Group.
 賦 予 有 限 度 授 權 的 管 理 架 構，旨 在 協 助 本 集        The system includes a defined management structure with limits of
 團 識 別 及 管 理 重 大 風 險，以 達 成 本 集 團 的 整        authority, and is designed to help the Group to identify and manage
 體業務目標，釐定本集團的風險狀況，監督風                       significant risks to achieve the Group’s overall business objectives,
 險管理框架結構，審核主要風險及緩解戰略，                       determine the Group’s risk profile, oversee risk management
 以及保障風險管理的成效及遵守相關法律法規。                      framework structure, review key risks and mitigation strategies and
                                            to ensure risk management effectiveness and compliance with
                                            relevant laws and regulations.

 本 集 團 利 用 自 下 而 上 及 自 上 而 下 的 方 式，確        A bottom-up and top-down approach is utilised to ensure a holistic
 保 擁 有 一 套 完 善 的 風 險 管 理 程 序。自 下 而 上        risk management process. The bottom-up approach is supported
 的方式以核心業務單位識別風險及確定風險                        by key business units to identify and prioritise risks while the top-
 評 級 為 基 礎；而 自 上 而 下 的 方 式 則 評 估 及 檢        down approach reviews and assesses if risks are comprehensively
 討核心業務單位於實現本集團的目標時是否                        identified, prioritised and properly addressed by key business units
 已 全 面 識 別 風 險、為 風 險 劃 分 級 別 及 予 以 妥        to accomplish the Group’s objectives. The management team
 善 處 理。管 理 層 團 隊 為 持 續 評 估 風 險 定 期 監        regularly monitors emerging risks for continuous risk assessment
 測 新 出 現 的 風 險，建 立 以 風 險 管 理 為 基 礎 的        purpose and for building the risk-management based internal audit
 內 部 審 核 計 劃，並 以 現 時 的 風 險 擔 保 為 目 的        plan and periodically assesses the adequacy and effectiveness of
 週期性評估風險管理及內部控制的適當性及                        risk management and internal controls for ongoing risk assurance
 有 效 性。此 舉 提 高 了 本 集 團 風 險 管 理 及 控 制        purposes. This enhances the effectiveness of the Group’s risk
 框架的有效性。                                    management and control framework.

本 公 司 內 部 審 計 單 位 隸 屬 董 事 會，在 董 事 會        The internal audit unit of the Company, which is subordinated to the

(2)   股東向董事會查詢                               (2)   Shareholders’ enquiries put to the Board

      股東可向本公司之香港股份過戶登記分                            Shareholders’ enquiries about their shareholdings can be

 我 們 的 目 標，是 對 綜 合 財 務 報 表 整 體 是 否 不        Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether
 存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述                        the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from
 取 得 合 理 保 證，並 出 具 包 括 我 們 意 見 的 核 數        material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue
 師 報 告。我 們 僅 向 閣 下（作 為 整 體）報 告，除            an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion
 此 之 外 本 報 告 別 無 其 他 目 的。我 們 不 會 就 本        solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not
 報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔任何                        assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person
 責任。合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證                       for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level
 按 照《香 港 審 計 準 則》進 行 的 審 計，在 某 一 重          of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in
 大 錯 誤 陳 述 存 在 時 總 能 發 現。錯 誤 陳 述 可 以        accordance with HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement
 由 欺 詐 或 錯 誤 引 起，如 果 合 理 預 期 它 們 單 獨        when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are
 或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依                        considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could
 賴 綜 合 財 務 報 表 所 作 出 的 經 濟 決 定，則 有 關        reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users
 的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。                               taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.

 在根據《香港審計準則》進行審計的過程中，                       As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise
 我們運用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。                       professional judgment and maintain professional scepticism

     及獲取充足和適當的審計憑證，作為我                           those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and
     們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串謀、                          appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of
     偽 造、蓄 意 遺 漏、虛 假 陳 述，或 淩 駕 於                 not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is

    2.1 編製基準                                   2.1 Basis of preparation

       本集團之綜合財務報表乃根據香港                              The consolidated financial statements of the Group have
       會計師公會頒佈的香港財務報告準                              been prepared in accordance with Hong Kong Financial
       則（「香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則」）及 香 港                    Reporting Standards (the “HKFRS”) issued by the Hong
      《公司條例》的披露規定編製。該等                              Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants and the
       綜合財務報表已按歷史成本基準編                              disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies
       製，以公平值計算的若干金融資產                              Ordinance. They have been prepared on a historical cost
       及 負 債（包 括 衍 生 工 具）或 以 公 平                    basis, except for certain financial assets and liabilities
       值計算的定額福利退休計劃之重新                              (including derivative instruments) measured at fair value
       估值金額及計劃資產除外。                                 or revalued amount and plan assets of defined benefit
                                                    pension plans measured at fair value.

      編製符合香港財務報告準則之財務                               The preparation of financial statements in conformity with
      報表需要使用若干關鍵會計估算。                               HKFRS requires the use of certain critical accounting

      政策過程中作出判斷。涉及高度之                               its judgment in the process of applying the Group’s

      對綜合財務報表作出重大假設和估                               of judgment or complexity, or areas where assumptions
      算之範疇，乃於附註 4 中披露。                              and estimates are significant to the consolidated financial
                                                    statements are disclosed in Note 4.

 2    重要會計政策概要（續）                           2    SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING
                                                 POLICIES (CONTINUED)

      2.2 附屬公司                                   2.2 Subsidiaries

         2.2.1 綜合賬目                                   2.2.1 Consolidation

         附屬公司指本集團具有控制權之實                               A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity)
         體（包括結構實體）。本集團就參與                              over which the Group has control. The Group controls
         該實體之可變回報承受風險或擁有                               an entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights to,
         權利，並有能力通過對該實體之影                               variable returns from its involvement with the entity and
         響力影響有關回報時，即屬對該實                               has the ability to affect those returns through its power
         體有控制權。附屬公司由控制權轉                               over the entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated from the

         賬。

      後續成本僅會在與項目有關之未來                              Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s

      2.12 抵銷金融工具                                2.12 Offsetting financial instruments

         債表列報之淨額。法律上可強制執                               the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The

         必須為正常業務中及於公司或對手                               events and must be enforceable in the normal course

      2.13 存貨                                    2.13 Inventories

         存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中之                               Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net
         較低者列賬。成本值以加權平均法                               realisable value. Cost is determined using the weighted
         計算。成品及在製品之成本包括原                               average method. The cost of finished goods and work
         材料、直接勞工及其他直接成本及                               in progress comprises raw materials, direct labour, other
         相關生產經常開支（按日常運營能                               direct costs and related production overheads (based
         力 計），不 包 括 借 貸 成 本。可 變 現                      on normal operating capacity). It excludes borrowing
         淨值是以日常業務之估計售價減去                               costs. Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in
         適用之變動銷售開支後所得之數額。                              the ordinary course of business, less applicable variable
                                                       selling expenses.

      2.14 應收貿易及其他賬款                             2.14 Trade and other receivables

         應收貿易賬款為在日常業務活動中                               Trade receivables are amounts due from customers for
         就商品銷售或服務執行而應收客戶                               merchandise sold or services performed in the ordinary
         之款項。如應收貿易及其他賬款之                               course of business. If collection of trade and other
         收 回 預 期 在 1 年 或 以 內（如 仍 在 正                   receivables is expected in one year or less (or in the
         常 經 營 週 期 中，則 可 較 長 時 間），                     normal operating cycle of the business if longer), they are
         其被分類為流動資產；否則按非流                               classified as current assets. If not, they are presented as
         動資產呈列。                                        non-current assets.

      2.18 借貸                                    2.18 Borrowings

         易成本後確認。借貸其後按攤銷成                               transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently

         法於借貸期間內在損益表確認。                                value is recognised in the income statement over the
                                                       period of the borrowings using the effective interest

         設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部                                Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are
         分或全部融資將會很有可能提取，                               recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the extent
         該費用可遞延確認為交易成本，直                               that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be
         至貸款提取為止。如沒有證據證明                               drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the
         部分或全部融資將會很有可能被提                               draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence
         取，則該項費用資本化作為流動資                               that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be
         金服務的預付款，並按有關融資期                               drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for
         間攤銷。                                          liquidity services and amortised over the period of the
                                                       facility to which it relates.

         除非本集團有無條件權利於報告期                               Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the
         末 後 將 負 債 的 結 算 遞 延 最 少 12 個                  Group has an unconditional right to defer settlement of
         月，否則借貸分類為流動負債。                                the liability for at least 12 months after the end of the

    2.22 撥備                                     2.22 Provisions

       倘本集團需就過去事項承擔現有法                               Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present
       律或推定責任，而有可能導致資源                               legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events;
       流出以履行該責任，並能可靠估計                               it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required
       金額才會確認撥備。並無就未來經                               to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably
       營虧損確認撥備。                                      estimated. Provisions are not recognised for future
                                                     operating losses.

       倘出現多項類似債務，會否導致經                               Where there are a number of similar obligations, the
       濟利益流出以清償債務乃經考慮債                               likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement is
       務之整體類別後確定。即使同類別                               determined by considering the class of obligations as a
       債務中任何一項可能流出經濟利益                               whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of
       之機會不大，仍會確認撥備。                                 an outflow with respect to any one item included in the
                                                     same class of obligations may be small.

    2.27 持作出售的非流動資產                          2.27 Non-current assets held for sale

      過出售交易而非持續使用而收回，                             carrying amount will be recovered principally through a

      資產將分類為持作出售。其按賬面                             a sale is considered highly probable. They are measured

      量，但遞延所得稅資產、自員工福                             costs to sell, except for assets such as deferred tax
      利產生的資產、以公平值計量的金                             assets, assets arising from employee benefits, financial
      融資產及投資性物業等訂明可獲豁                             assets and investment property that are carried at fair
      免遵守該規定的資產則除外。                               value and contractual rights under insurance contracts,
                                                  which are specifically exempt from this requirement.

      減值虧損乃於資產初始或其後撇                              An impairment loss is recognised for any initial or
      減至公平值扣除出售成本時確認。                             subsequent write-down of the asset to fair value less
      當資產公平值減出售成本其後有任                             costs to sell. A gain is recognised for any subsequent
      何增加時，即確認利得，惟金額不                             increases in fair value less costs to sell of an asset,
      得超過先前確認的任何累計減值虧                             but not in excess of any cumulative impairment loss
      損。於非流動資產的出售日期前並                             previously recognised. A gain or loss not previously
      未確認的利得或虧損於終止確認日                             recognised by the date of the sale of the non-current
      期確認。                                        asset is recognised at the date of derecognition.

      非流動資產在分類為持作出售時不                             Non-current assets are not depreciated or amortised

      2.28 或然負債及或然資產                             2.28 Contingent liabilities and contingent assets

         或然負債乃一項因過往事件可能產                               A contingent liability is a possible obligation that arises
         生的承擔，而該等過往事件的存在                               from past events and whose existence will only be
         僅可由一項或多項並非由本集團全                               confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one
         權控制的日後不明朗事件的存在與                               or more uncertain future events not wholly within the
         否確定。或然負債亦可為一項因不                               control of the Group. It can also be a present obligation
         大可能需要耗用經濟資源或承擔之                               arising from past events that is not recognised because
         金額未能可靠地計算而未確認之過                               it is not probable that outflow of economic resources
         往事件產生之現有承擔。                                   will be required or the amount of obligation cannot be
                                                       measured reliably.

         或然負債未予以確認，惟已於財務                               A contingent liability is not recognised but is disclosed in
         報表中披露。倘耗用經濟資源之可                               the financial statements. When a change in the probability
         能性出現變動致使有可能需耗用經                               of an outflow occurs so that outflow is probable, it will
         濟資源，則或然負債將確認為撥備。                              then be recognised as a provision.

         或然資產指因為已發生的事件而可                               A contingent asset is a possible asset that arises from
         能產生的資產，此等資產只能根據                               past events and whose existence will be confirmed only
         本集團不能完全控制的一項或多項                               by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more
         不確定事件的出現與否方能確認。                               uncertain events not wholly within the control of the
                                                       Group.

         或然資產未予確認，惟於可能出現                               A contingent asset is not recognised but is disclosed in
         經濟利益流入時於財務報表附註中                               the notes to the financial statements when an inflow of
         披露。若經濟利益流入可被實質確                               economic benefits is probable. When inflow is virtually
         定時，則對該資產進行確認。                                 certain, an asset is recognised.

      2.29 股息分派                                  2.29 Dividend distribution

         分派予本公司股東之股息乃於股息                               Dividend distribution to the Company’s shareholders
         獲本公司之股東批准之期間於本集                               is recognised as a liability in the Group’s and the
         團及本公司財務報表中確認為負債。                              Company’s financial statements in the period in which the
                                                       dividends are approved by the Company’s shareholders.

    在有關情況下對未來事件的合理預測。                           expectations of future events that are believed to be reasonable
                                                under the circumstances.

    4.1 關鍵會計估算及假設                               4.1 Critical accounting estimates and assumptions

      本集團對未來作出估算及假設。據                                The Group makes estimates and assumptions
      定義，由此而達致之會計估算甚少                                concerning the future. The resulting accounting estimates
      與有關之實際結果相同。極有可能                                will, by definition, seldom equal the related actual results.
      導致下個財政年度內之資產及負債                                The estimates and assumptions that have a significant
      賬面值出現重大調整之估算及假設                                risk of causing a material adjustment to the carrying
      現討論如下。                                         amounts of assets and liabilities within the next financial
                                                     year are discussed below.

      (a)   所得稅及遞延所得稅資產                              (a)   Income taxes and deferred income tax
                                                           assets

      本集團主要需於中國繳納所得稅。                                The Group is mainly subject to income taxes in the
      在確定所得稅之撥備時須作出重大                                PRC. Significant judgment is required in determining the
      判斷。很多交易及計算所涉及的最                                provision for income taxes. There are many transactions
      終稅務釐定都是不確定的。本集團                                and calculations for which the ultimate tax determination
      須估計未來會否須繳納額外稅項，                                is uncertain. The Group recognises liabilities for
      以就預期稅務審核事宜確認負債。                                anticipated tax audit issues based on estimates of
      倘該等事宜之最終稅務結果與起初                                whether additional taxes will be due. Where the final tax
      記錄入賬之金額不同，該等差額將                                outcome of these matters is different from the amounts
      會影響釐定稅項期間之當期及遞延                                that were initially recorded, such differences will impact
      所得稅資產及負債。                                      the current and deferred income tax assets and liabilities
                                                     in the period in which such determination is made.

      有關若干暫時性差異及稅項虧損之                                Deferred income tax assets relating to certain
      遞延所得稅項資產已予確認，原因                                temporary differences and tax losses are recognised as
      為管理層認為可取得未來應課稅溢                                management considers it is probable that future taxable
      利，以用於抵銷可抵扣暫時性差異                                profit will be available against which the deductible
      或稅項虧損。                                         temporary differences or tax losses can be utilized.

  (iii) 本集團之租賃活動及有關活動                      (iii) The Group’s leasing activities and how they are
        之入賬方式                                    accounted for

    本集團之土地使用權全部均已悉數                             The Group’s land use rights are all fully prepaid.
    預付。此外，本集團租賃多個辦公                             In addition, the Group leases various offices and
    室及倉庫。租約一般以固定期限三                             warehouses. Rental contracts are typically made for
    個月至十年訂立，惟可能涵蓋如下                             fixed periods of three months to ten years, but may have
    文 (iv) 所述延期選擇權。                             extension options as described in (iv) below.

    租賃期按個別情況磋商，並包含各                             Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and
    種不同條款及條件。除出租人持有                             contain a wide range of different terms and conditions.
    之租賃資產中的抵押權益外，租賃                             The lease agreements do not impose any covenants
    協議並無施加任何限制，惟租賃資                             other than the security interests in the leased assets that
    產不得用作借貸的抵押。                                 are held by the lessor. Leased assets may not be used
                                                as security for borrowing purposes.

  (iv) 延期及終止選擇權                            (iv) Extension and termination options

    本集團的若干物業及設備租賃包含                             Extension and termination options are included in a
    延期及終止選擇權。該等條款乃用                             number of property and equipment leases across the
    於就管理合約令經營靈活性最大                              Group. These terms are used to maximise operational
    化。所持有的大部分延期及終止選                             flexibility in terms of managing contracts. The majority of
    擇權僅可由本集團行使，惟不得由                             extension and termination options held are exercisable
    有關出租人行使。                                    only by the Group and not by the respective lessor.

  價值變動風險，惟本集團一般會訂立新                              at the end of the current leases, the Group typically enters into
  經營租賃，因此不會即時變現目前租賃                              new operating leases and therefore will not immediately realise
  完結時之任何剩餘價值減少。有關未來                              any reduction in residual value at the end of these leases.

  (a)   繳入盈餘                                     (a)   Contribution surplus

        本公司的繳入盈餘主要指本公司股                                Contribution surplus of the Company mainly represent
        份上市之前本公司權益持有人根據                                the net balance of contributions from and distributions
        就上市已進行之重組所作出之注資                                to the equity holder of the Company prior to the listing of
        及向彼等作出分派的結餘淨額。                                 shares of the Company pursuant to the reorganisation
                                                       for the purpose of the Listing.

        按照開曼群島法律與法規，該等繳                                According to the law and regulation of the Cayman
        入盈餘可分派予本公司的權益持有                                Islands, such contribution surplus is distributable to the
        人。                                             equity holders of the Company.

  (b)   法定儲備                                     (b)   Statutory reserves

        中國法律與法規要求在中國註冊的                                The PRC laws and regulations require companies
        公司，在對權益持有人作溢利分派                                registered in the PRC to provide for certain statutory
        前從各自的法定財務報表所申報的                                reserves, which are appropriated from the net profit as
        溢利淨額（在抵銷以往年度的累計                                reported in their respective statutory financial statements
        虧損後）中提撥一定的法定儲備。                                after offsetting accumulated losses from prior years and
        所有法定儲備乃就特定目的而增設。                               before profit distributions to equity holder. All statutory
                                                       reserves are created for specific purposes.

        作為外商獨資企業和內資企業註冊                                PRC subsidiaries incorporated as wholly-foreign owned
        成立的中國附屬公司，在進行當年                                enterprises and domestic companies are required to
        度除稅後溢利分派時，須將法定溢                                appropriate 10% of statutory net profits to statutory
        利 淨 額 之 10% 撥 作 法 定 盈 餘 公 積                    surplus reserves, upon distribution of their post-tax profits
        金，直至法定盈餘公積金不少於公                                of the current year, until the statutory surplus reserve is
        司之註冊資本 50% 為止。此外，此                             not less than 50% of its registered capital. In addition, at
        等公司可在各自董事會酌情決定下                                the discretion of the respective boards of directors, these
        將部份除稅後溢利撥入職工福利及                                companies may allocate a portion of their post-tax profits
        花紅儲備及任意盈餘公積金。作為                                to the staff welfare and bonus reserve and discretionary
        中外合資經營企業註冊成立的中國                                surplus reserve. PRC subsidiaries incorporated as sino-
        附屬公司，在其各自董事會酌情決                                foreign equity joint venture companies may allocate a
        定下將部份法定除稅後溢利撥入法                                portion of their statutory post-tax profits to the statutory
        定儲備。                                           reserves at the discretion of the respective boards of
                                                       directors.

        法定盈餘公積金只能用於彌補公司                                The statutory surplus reserves shall only be used to make
        的虧損、擴大公司的生產經營或增                                up losses of the companies, to expand the companies’
        加公司資本。                                         production operations, or to increase the capital of the
                                                       companies.

 37 期後事項                                                      37 SUBSEQUENT EVENT

      於 2023 年 3 月 3 日，本 公 司 全 資 附 屬 公                              On 3 March 2023, 合 肥 統 一 企 業 有 限 公 司 (Hefei President
      司合肥統一企業有限公司（「合肥統一」）                                           Enterprises Co., Ltd.*) (“Hefei President”), a wholly owned
      與合肥土地儲備中心（「土地儲備中心」）                                           subsidiary of the Group, and 合 肥 市 土 地 儲 備 中 心 (Hefei
      訂 立 土 地 使 用 權 出 售 協 議，據 此，土 地                                 Land Reserve Centre*) (the “Land Reserve Centre”), entered
      儲備中心已同意收購而合肥統一已同意                                             into a land use right disposal agreement pursuant to which
